# Glue mask before / after -- per animal x epoch (generated 2026-11-10 21:06)

Generated by `scripts/glue_before_after.py` (read-only on M:). Re-run it as more `_g` steps finish; it rewrites this file. Glue-in = the 11-03 rebuild WITHOUT the painted fiber-glue mask; glue-out = queue v20 re-run on main 8aed3a7b. Every readout below is a change from the animal's OWN pre first; pooled = mean over animals; stars = `boot_delta` CI (95 %, paired animals -> sessions). Thresholds: large = |change| > 25 % and >= 0.01; sign flip counted when one side is >= 0.005 from 0.

**Expected to change:** SSs (~50 % glue), VIS, AUD, TEa, RSPagl_L, whole-cortex means; glue-only areas dropped. **Expected stable:** ALM, tjM1, MOs / MOp, SSp-m / SSp-n / SSp-un (and LocaNMF decoders).

**Caveat:** the glue-in runs used older code (11-05 .. 11-08 commits, listed per step); a difference can also come from code / input changes between the two runs, not only from the mask.
